> **How to read this table:** `adj` is quality estimate `mu+alpha` with `SE=1.194/√n` and `lower=adj−1.96·SE`; `resid=adj−expected` Q3b/OLS (model-dependent conditional anomaly); `min_alt` is minimum resid across Q3b/Q4/WLS variants; `users`/`rank` are recognition (popularity, not broad appeal); `weights` is attention proxy; disposition is single-label priority — see `candidate_dispositions.md` for distributions. Full evidence per candidate below.

## `likely_hidden_gem_candidate` — 55 strongest plausible hidden-gem candidates for manual review

> Each candidate below reports **four dimensions separately** plus major caveats. **Do not claim broad appeal established where data cannot.** Treat as candidates for external validation, not proof. Heavy-vs-light gap is severity not taste; category breadth is proxy not diversity; share_own is snapshot-time (58% everywhere, PR#4).

### 1. Heart of Crown: Fairy Garden — `game_id 156372` (2013.0; n=305 decile D6)
**Disposition:** `likely_hidden_gem_candidate` — heavy mean 7.52>=7.5 with n_low 43 vs n_high 65 and share_own 0.702 in [0.45,0.78]

**Quality (dim 1):** `adj 8.43` = `mu 7.144` + `alpha 1.29`; `SE 0.068` (`1.194/√305`) `post_SD 0.068` `z=22.8` `lb 8.30` `lower_1 8.36` `lower_post 8.30` — **top quartile** (`adj≥7.5` P75=7.515) — SE-aware: `adj 7.5 at n=200 lb7.335` vs same adj at n=3000 lb7.54; do not treat point estimate alone.

**Underratedness (dim 2):** `resid 1.56` = `adj 8.43` − `E[adj] 6.88` Q3b/OLS (46 feat, CV R² .582); `min_alt 1.53` stable across Q3b/Q4/WLS; **model-dependent conditional anomaly**, not latent quality; volume band-flat by construction (`corr resid, log n −0.004`).

**Recognition / hiddenness (dim 3):** `users_rated` 350 (`rank 4946`) — **popularity, not broad appeal**; `num_weights` 14 (attention proxy, median 20 population, 29 gated); `is_reimplementation` False (family reach reimpl avg users 7,338 vs 1,619 non-reimpl — reach ≠ broad appeal); **sufficiently obscure** (`users<5000` & `rank≥1000` or null) — not well-known (0 of 544 meet users≥20000 or rank<500).

**Audience evidence (dim 4):** `share_heavy_500plus` 0.213 (`share_heavy_250plus 0.439`) / `share_light_10_24` 0.056 / `mean(delta)` -0.421 (severity level, not credibility; SD 0.177); `share_own` 0.702 (snapshot-time, PR#4, pop mean 0.571±0.145 — moderate, not high 0.84+ niche); heavy vs light on same game where both rated `10-49 vs 500+` `has_diff True` `mean_low 8.544186046511628` vs `mean_high 7.516923076923077` `diff 1.0272629695885511` (where n≥10 both sides; positive diff = light raters higher = severity, not taste; **suggestive cross-audience consistency requires heavy mean≥7.5**); `cat` Card Game; Fantasy (category breadth mean 2.77 — tag overlap descriptive, not causal).

**Major caveats:** share_own snapshot-time 58% everywhere (not longitudinal); country 27.3% missing (not per-game here, see `broad_appeal_evidence.md` for global 72.7% coverage); rank unranked for 98/544 gated; `SE` at n=305 is 0.068 vs median 0.070 and P90 0.023 (10× span) — larger n more precise but self-selection (who is in sample) not corrected by SE; no external sales/plays validation; `users_rated` is popularity, not breadth; residual is conditional anomaly, not broad appeal; **niche `share_own 0.84–0.87` not present here** (those 19 are separate disposition).

---

### 2. 1841: Railways in Northern Italy — `game_id 1447` (1994.0; n=343 decile D6)

## `high_quality_but_well_known` — 0 games among gated (all 544 below `users≥20000` or `rank<500`)

> Strict well-known threshold captures 530 flagged wellknown in Phase 7 (67 met robust criteria) — all were excluded before the quality gate; the 544 gated have 0 meeting users≥20000 or rank<500. Looser `insufficient_hiddenness` (users≥5000 or rank<1000) captures 40 that are not obscure enough for hidden-gem status.

---

*Tagging per AGENTS.md:* counts are observed facts; thresholds are method choices; resid/adj/expected are model-dependent Q3b/OLS; hiddenness is popularity proxy not broad appeal; audience evidence is severity-level not taste; category `cat_str` is tag overlap not audience diversity; no external validation.
